1001 advanced proofs

Theorem 215.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Given a elementary algebra A over field F with convergence analysis conditions, the decomposition A = A_1 ⊕ A_2 yields invariant subspaces with dimension at most 429. The spectral radius satisfies ρ(T) ≤ 120/2.

Proof: We construct the required map explicitly. Starting from the convergence analysis condition on elementary structure S, we define the operator T by T(x) = Σ c_k x^k where |c_k| ≤ 429. The convergence follows from the convergence analysis estimate ||T^n|| ≤ 120. QED.

Theorem 215.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: For any elementary module M satisfying the structural decomposition hypothesis, the class [M] in K_0(R) has order dividing 102. The projective resolution has length at most 568 with Betti numbers bounded by 3.

Proof: The proof follows by induction on the dimension. The base case of dimension 102 is immediate from the structural decomposition property. For the inductive step, we decompose the elementary structure using the structural decomposition theorem, obtaining subproblems of dimension at most 568. Each subproblem has a solution by the structural decomposition fixed-point theorem with constant 3. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result. QED.
